# All bass multi-effects — comparison

[Guitar](ALL-GUITAR.md) · [Bass](ALL-BASS.md) · [Vocal](ALL-VOCAL.md) · [Category](MULTI-EFFECTS.md) · [Repository](README.md)

73 recorded bass-focused or explicitly documented bass-capable multi-effects: 20 bass-focused and 53 shared models. This compares actual model records, not only bass-branded units. Capability evidence is listed below; it does not establish equal low-end retention or every effect working equally well on bass.

These are **comparisons of the recorded catalogue**, not exhaustive market coverage or fresh stock certification. Organized 2026-10-16 from the linked source records; their original technical/price dates, firmware, revision and access limits still apply. Every model remains partial. **? means unverified, not No.** Read the complete linked record for sources and conflicting evidence; this view does not silently resolve those conflicts.

K/F/B = independently adjustable rotary controls / footswitches / separate buttons; toe and power switches are additional unless explicitly stated. Body axes and mass qualifications are retained. Color does not prove touch; USB audio does not prove bus power; IRs, native NAM, converted NAM and proprietary captures differ.

Tuner,snapshots,trails and USB MIDI are documented; an internal drum machine is not established. Manufacturer describes snapshot changes without audio dropouts or disruption of delay/reverb trails; this is not a measured whole-device latency or blanket preset-switching guarantee. PG-M,PG-Y,PG-P<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** Line 6 specifies **up to30 m/100 ft line-of-sight** and about **seven hours transmitter playing time**, both manufacturer ratings rather than independent tests. The floorboard needs its own supply. RF antennas are above the expression-pedal toe area. Wireless reliability depends on RF conditions; do not cover the antenna or assume arbitrary crowded2.4 GHz conditions meet the rated range. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** GUITAR IN charges the transmitter; the separate rear well stores it. Leaving the transmitter in the charge port while the processor is off slowly discharges it, so the storage well is the documented storage location. Wireless accepts Guitar,Wireless or Guitar+Wireless input selections; ordinary POD Go presets are compatible and their Guitar input is automatically converted to Guitar+Wireless when imported. USB3/4 is a reamp source; USB1/2 is monitoring that bypasses processing. Cable Tone and Wireless Gain are separate settings, not additional audio-effect blocks. PG-W<br>**Wireless-specific operation:** The [WirelessFAQ](https://kb.line6.com/pod-go-wireless-faq),rechecked2026-10-17,recommends resyncing in each new room: insert the transmitter intoGUITAR IN,wait **10–15seconds**,then reconnect to the guitar. Keep at least **3m** from2.4GHz Wi-Fi routers. Up to **four systems** are described with sequential scanning while earlier transmitters stay awake;after **four minutes** without audio the transmitter sleeps. These are conditional manufacturer instructions,not a venue reliability guarantee or floorboard battery rating. PG-W |

Offered unit version unknown; host compatibility does not establish USB3 transfer speed. — Evidence: [Firmware history](https://www.zoom.co.jp/sites/default/files/products/downloads/pdfs/B3_v1.20_E.pdf) |

This establishes model-specific bass support without relying on HD500X or later paid packs. [Official guide](https://l6c-acdn2.line6.net/data/6/0a06434dc1a55085c5d6532bf/application/pdf/POD%20HD500%20Advanced%20Guide%20v2.10%20%28%20Rev%20A%20%29.pdf). |

## Bass classification still unresolved

These other guitar/acoustic records have **not been certified bass-capable or unsuitable** by this pass. They stay outside the positive bass comparison until model-specific evidence establishes the intended processing path; a BASS EQ knob or sibling product is insufficient.

## Scope and provenance

Instrument views overlap and must not be summed. All 181 active models count once in `research-state.json`; the six explicitly excluded archives never enter these tables. Former/donated eligible devices remain without private receipt/order/payment details. Root brand records take precedence where consolidated; legacy sources remain for full evidence until a checked lossless merge. No GitHub workflow is used.
